    // Requires the real Postgres/PostgREST/Redis test stack with 001_schema.sql
    // applied. Unit mocks cannot validate trigram operators, indexes, or SQL scope.
    const { Pool } = require('pg');
    const { supabaseAdmin } = require('../../../src/config/supabase');
    const { findMembers, findContainers } = require('../../../src/services/search.service');
    const { seedWorkspaceWithAdmin, cleanupWorkspace } = require('../../helpers/dbHelper');
    const { buildProxyMember, buildContainer } = require('../../fixtures/factories');
    
    describe('ranked entity search with Postgres', () => {
      const workspaceIds = [];
    
      async function seedTenant() {
        const tenant = await seedWorkspaceWithAdmin(supabaseAdmin, { member: { display_name: 'Xylophone' } });
        workspaceIds.push(tenant.workspace.id);
        return tenant;
      }
    
      async function addMember(workspaceId, name, overrides = {}) {
        const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from('workspace_members').insert(buildProxyMember({
          workspace_id: workspaceId, display_name: name, ...overrides,
        })).select().single();
        if (error) throw error;
        return data;
      }
    
      async function addContainer(tenant, name, overrides = {}) {
        const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from('containers').insert(buildContainer({
          workspace_id: tenant.workspace.id, created_by: tenant.member.id, name, ...overrides,
        })).select().single();
        if (error) throw error;
        return data;
      }
    
      afterAll(async () => {
        for (const workspaceId of workspaceIds) await cleanupWorkspace(supabaseAdmin, workspaceId);
      });
    
      it('finds a misspelled first name within a full member name', async () => {
        const { workspace } = await seedTenant();
        const member = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze Okafor');
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: workspace.id, query: 'Adaze' });
        expect(result.status).toBe('single_match');
        expect(result.candidates).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ id: member.id, match_type: 'fuzzy', is_proxy: true })]);
        expect(result.candidates[0].score).toBeGreaterThan(0.3);
        expect(result.candidates[0].score).toBeLessThan(1);
      });
    
      it('ranks exact names before substrings before fuzzy matches, keeping all ambiguous', async () => {
        const { workspace } = await seedTenant();
        const fuzzy = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaze');
        const substring = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze Okafor');
        const exact = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze');
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: workspace.id, query: 'adaeze' });
        expect(result.status).toBe('ambiguous');
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([exact.id, substring.id, fuzzy.id]);
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.match_type)).toEqual(['exact', 'substring', 'fuzzy']);
      });
    
      it('excludes inactive, deleted, and other-workspace members', async () => {
        const first = await seedTenant();
        const second = await seedTenant();
        const active = await addMember(first.workspace.id, 'Adaeze');
        await addMember(first.workspace.id, 'Adaeze', { is_active: false });
        await addMember(first.workspace.id, 'Adaeze', { deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() });
        await addMember(second.workspace.id, 'Adaeze');
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: first.workspace.id, query: 'Adaeze' });
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([active.id]);
      });
    
      it('uses a deterministic order for duplicate names and reports truncated ambiguity', async () => {
        const { workspace } = await seedTenant();
        const members = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) members.push(await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze'));
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: workspace.id, query: 'Adaeze', limit: 2 });
        expect(result.status).toBe('ambiguous');
        expect(result.has_more).toBe(true);
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual(members.map((member) => member.id).sort().slice(0, 2));
      });
    
      it('finds typo matches in container names and preserves exact-match priority', async () => {
        const tenant = await seedTenant();
        const fuzzy = await addContainer(tenant, 'Adaeze rent pool');
        const exact = await addContainer(tenant, 'Adaze');
        const result = await findContainers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query: 'Adaze' });
        expect(result.status).toBe('ambiguous');
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([exact.id, fuzzy.id]);
        expect(result.candidates[1].match_type).toBe('fuzzy');
      });
    
      it('includes archived containers but excludes deleted and other-workspace containers', async () => {
        const first = await seedTenant();
        const second = await seedTenant();
        const archived = await addContainer(first, 'Rent', { status: 'archived' });
        await addContainer(first, 'Rent', { deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() });
        await addContainer(second, 'Rent');
        const result = await findContainers({ workspaceId: first.workspace.id, query: 'Rent' });
        expect(result.candidates).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ id: archived.id, status: 'archived' })]);
      });
    
      it.each(['%_', '\\_'])('treats short ILIKE metacharacters literally: %s', async (query) => {
        const tenant = await seedTenant();
        const member = await addMember(tenant.workspace.id, `Member ${query} literal`);
        const container = await addContainer(tenant, `Container ${query} literal`);
        await addMember(tenant.workspace.id, 'Unrelated');
        await addContainer(tenant, 'Unrelated');
        const members = await findMembers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query });
        const containers = await findContainers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query });
        expect(members.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([member.id]);
        expect(containers.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([container.id]);
      });
    
      it('returns no match for an unrelated name or a deleted workspace', async () => {
        const tenant = await seedTenant();
        await addMember(tenant.workspace.id, 'Adaeze');
        await addContainer(tenant, 'Rent');
        expect((await findMembers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query: 'Zqvjkx' })).status).toBe('not_found');
        expect((await findContainers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query: 'Zqvjkx' })).status).toBe('not_found');
        const { error } = await supabaseAdmin.from('workspaces').update({ deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', tenant.workspace.id);
        if (error) throw error;
        expect((await findMembers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query: 'Adaeze' })).status).toBe('not_found');
        expect((await findContainers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query: 'Rent' })).status).toBe('not_found');
      });
    
      it('has indexes and keeps the RPCs unavailable to PUBLIC and existing browser roles', async () => {
        const pool = new Pool({ connectionString: process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL || 'postgres://postgres:test@localhost:54322/kith_test' });
        try {
          const { rows: functions } = await pool.query(`
            SELECT p.oid, p.prosecdef, p.proacl FROM pg_proc p
            JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.pronamespace
            WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND p.proname IN ('search_members_ranked', 'search_containers_ranked')
          `);
          expect(functions).toHaveLength(2);
          for (const fn of functions) {
            expect(fn.prosecdef).toBe(false);
            const { rows: publicGrants } = await pool.query('SELECT * FROM aclexplode($1::aclitem[]) WHERE grantee = 0', [fn.proacl]);
            expect(publicGrants).toEqual([]);
            const { rows: browserGrants } = await pool.query(`
              SELECT rolname, has_function_privilege(oid, $1::oid, 'EXECUTE') AS can_execute
              FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname IN ('anon', 'authenticated')
            `, [fn.oid]);
            browserGrants.forEach((role) => expect(role.can_execute).toBe(false));
          }
          const { rows: indexes } = await pool.query(`
            SELECT indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = 'public'
            AND indexname IN ('idx_workspace_members_display_name_trgm', 'idx_containers_name_trgm')
          `);
          expect(indexes).toHaveLength(2);
          indexes.forEach((index) => expect(index.indexdef).toMatch(/USING gin/));
        } finally {
          await pool.end();
        }
      });
    });

    function makeContext(role = 'member') {

        userId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',
        member: {
          id: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', role, displayName: 'Adaeze',
          isProxy: false, isActive: true, workspaceId: '33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333',
        },

      };
    }
    
    describe.each([
      ['find_member', findMember, searchService.findMembers],
      ['find_container', findContainer, searchService.findContainers],
    ])('%s resolution tool', (name, handler, service) => {
      beforeEach(() => service.mockReset());
    
      it.each(['member', 'admin'])('is a Tier 0 tool available to an authenticated %s', async (role) => {
        const context = makeContext(role);
        expect(getAvailableTools(context).find((tool) => tool.name === name)).toMatchObject({ tier: 0, minRole: 'member' });
        service.mockResolvedValue({ query: 'Adaeze', status: 'single_match', candidates: [], has_more: false });
        await executeReadTool({ name, args: { query: '  Adaeze  ' }, context });
        expect(service).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ workspaceId: context.workspace.id, query: 'Adaeze', limit: 10 });
      });
    
      it.each(['not_found', 'single_match', 'ambiguous'])('preserves the %s result without choosing a candidate', async (status) => {
        const candidates = status === 'not_found' ? [] : [{ id: 'candidate-a', score: 0.5 }];
        if (status === 'ambiguous') candidates.push({ id: 'candidate-b', score: 0.4 });
        const payload = { query: 'Adaze', status, candidates, has_more: false };
        service.mockResolvedValue(payload);
        const result = await executeReadTool({ name, args: { query: 'Adaze' }, context: makeContext() });
        expect(result).toBe(payload);
        expect(result).not.toHaveProperty('resolved_id');
      });
    
      it.each([

        { ...makeContext(), member: { ...makeContext().member, isActive: false } },

      ])('denies an invalid context even when calling the handler directly', async (context) => {

      role: 'member', is_proxy: true, match_type: 'fuzzy', score: 0.5,
    };
    const container = {
      id: '33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333', name: 'Rent',
      container_type: 'recurring', status: 'active', match_type: 'exact', score: 1,
    };
    
    describe.each([
      ['members', findMembers, 'search_members_ranked', member],
      ['containers', findContainers, 'search_containers_ranked', container],
    ])('ranked %s search', (label, find, rpcName, candidate) => {
      beforeEach(() => db.reset());
    
      it('scopes the RPC, trims the query, escapes wildcards, and requests a lookahead row', async () => {
        db.mockNextRpcResponse({ data: [], error: null });
        const result = await find({ workspaceId, query: '  50%_x\\  ' });
    
        expect(db.getRpcCalls()).toEqual([{
          fnName: rpcName,
          params: { p_workspace_id: workspaceId, p_query: '50%_x\\', p_pattern: '%50\\%\\_x\\\\%', p_limit: 11 },
        }]);
        expect(result).toEqual({ query: '50%_x\\', status: 'not_found', candidates: [], has_more: false });
      });
    
      it('returns a single candidate with only allowed fields and its original score', async () => {
        db.mockNextRpcResponse({ data: [{ ...candidate, admin_notes: 'secret', public_token: 'secret' }], error: null });
        const result = await find({ workspaceId, query: 'Adaze' });
    
        expect(result).toEqual({ query: 'Adaze', status: 'single_match', candidates: [candidate], has_more: false });
        expect(result).not.toHaveProperty('resolved_id');
      });
    
      it('preserves database ranking and marks multiple candidates ambiguous even with an exact first result', async () => {
        const first = { ...candidate, match_type: 'exact', score: 1 };
        const second = { ...candidate, id: '44444444-4444-4444-8444-444444444444', match_type: 'fuzzy', score: 0.4 };
        db.mockNextRpcResponse({ data: [first, second], error: null });
    
        await expect(find({ workspaceId, query: 'Adaeze', limit: 2 })).resolves.toEqual({
          query: 'Adaeze', status: 'ambiguous', candidates: [first, second], has_more: false,
        });
      });
    
      it('truncates the display list without hiding additional candidates', async () => {
        const rows = Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, index) => ({ ...candidate, id: `candidate-${index}` }));
        db.mockNextRpcResponse({ data: rows, error: null });
        const result = await find({ workspaceId, query: 'Adaeze', limit: 2 });
    
        expect(result).toEqual({ query: 'Adaeze', status: 'ambiguous', candidates: rows.slice(0, 2), has_more: true });
        expect(db.getRpcCalls()[0].params.p_limit).toBe(3);
      });
    
      it.each([
        { query: '' }, { query: '   ' }, { query: 'a' }, { query: null }, { query: 123 },
        { query: 'a'.repeat(201) }, { limit: 1 }, { limit: 21 }, { limit: 2.5 }, { limit: '10' },
        { workspaceId: 'invalid' }, { workspaceId: undefined },
      ])('rejects invalid parameters before reaching the database: %j', async (override) => {
        await expect(find({ workspaceId, query: 'Adaeze', ...override })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
        expect(db.getRpcCalls()).toEqual([]);
      });
    
      it.each([
        { data: null, error: { code: 'PGRST202', message: 'RPC missing with internal details' } },
        { data: null, error: null },
        { data: {}, error: null },
      ])('reports an unavailable search instead of a false no-match on RPC failure', async (response) => {
        db.mockNextRpcResponse(response);
        await expect(find({ workspaceId, query: 'Adaeze' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
          statusCode: 503, code: 'SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE', message: 'Entity search is unavailable. Please try again.',
        });
      });
    
      it('converts transport failures into the same typed availability error', async () => {
        db.client.rpc.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Network detail'));
        await expect(find({ workspaceId, query: 'Adaeze' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE' });
      });
    });

    CREATE INDEX idx_containers_public_token ON public.containers USING btree (public_token) WHERE (public_token IS NOT NULL);
    
    CREATE INDEX idx_containers_type ON public.containers USING btree (workspace_id, container_type) WHERE (deleted_at IS NULL);
    
    CREATE INDEX idx_containers_workspace ON public.containers USING btree (workspace_id, status) WHERE (deleted_at IS NULL);
    
    CREATE INDEX idx_containers_name_trgm ON public.containers
        USING gin (name gin_trgm_ops) WHERE (deleted_at IS NULL);
    
    CREATE TRIGGER containers_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.containers FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();
    
    -- Ranked entity resolution. search.service supplies p_pattern using the shared
    -- containsPattern() escaping helper and requests one extra row for has_more.
    -- The %> operator is word_similarity(p_query, name): it can match a misspelled
    -- first name inside a full name, and is supported by the GIN trigram indexes.
    -- Pin the threshold per call so connection/session settings cannot change it.
    CREATE FUNCTION public.search_members_ranked(
        p_workspace_id uuid, p_query text, p_pattern text, p_limit integer DEFAULT 11
    ) RETURNS TABLE (id uuid, display_name text, role text, is_proxy boolean, match_type text, score real)
        LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY INVOKER
        SET search_path TO pg_catalog, public, extensions, pg_temp
        SET pg_trgm.word_similarity_threshold TO '0.3'
        AS $$
        WITH matches AS (
            SELECT m.id, m.display_name, m.role, m.is_proxy,
                CASE WHEN lower(m.display_name) = lower(p_query) THEN 'exact'
                     WHEN m.display_name ILIKE p_pattern ESCAPE E'\\' THEN 'substring'
                     ELSE 'fuzzy' END AS match_type,
                greatest(similarity(p_query, m.display_name), word_similarity(p_query, m.display_name)) AS score
            FROM public.workspace_members m
            WHERE m.workspace_id = p_workspace_id
              AND m.is_active = true AND m.deleted_at IS NULL
              AND char_length(p_query) BETWEEN 2 AND 200
              AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.workspaces w WHERE w.id = p_workspace_id AND w.deleted_at IS NULL)
              AND (m.display_name ILIKE p_pattern ESCAPE E'\\'
                   OR (char_length(p_query) >= 3 AND m.display_name %> p_query))
        )
        SELECT m.id, m.display_name, m.role, m.is_proxy, m.match_type, m.score
        FROM matches m
        ORDER BY CASE m.match_type WHEN 'exact' THEN 0 WHEN 'substring' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END,
                 m.score DESC, lower(m.display_name) COLLATE "C", m.id
        LIMIT least(21, greatest(2, coalesce(p_limit, 11)));
    $$;
    
    CREATE FUNCTION public.search_containers_ranked(
        p_workspace_id uuid, p_query text, p_pattern text, p_limit integer DEFAULT 11
    ) RETURNS TABLE (id uuid, name text, container_type text, status text, match_type text, score real)
        LANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY INVOKER
        SET search_path TO pg_catalog, public, extensions, pg_temp
        SET pg_trgm.word_similarity_threshold TO '0.3'
        AS $$
        WITH matches AS (
            SELECT c.id, c.name, c.container_type, c.status,
                CASE WHEN lower(c.name) = lower(p_query) THEN 'exact'
                     WHEN c.name ILIKE p_pattern ESCAPE E'\\' THEN 'substring'
                     ELSE 'fuzzy' END AS match_type,
                greatest(similarity(p_query, c.name), word_similarity(p_query, c.name)) AS score
            FROM public.containers c
            WHERE c.workspace_id = p_workspace_id AND c.deleted_at IS NULL
              AND char_length(p_query) BETWEEN 2 AND 200
              AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.workspaces w WHERE w.id = p_workspace_id AND w.deleted_at IS NULL)
              AND (c.name ILIKE p_pattern ESCAPE E'\\'
                   OR (char_length(p_query) >= 3 AND c.name %> p_query))
        )
        SELECT c.id, c.name, c.container_type, c.status, c.match_type, c.score
        FROM matches c
        ORDER BY CASE c.match_type WHEN 'exact' THEN 0 WHEN 'substring' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END,
                 c.score DESC, lower(c.name) COLLATE "C", c.id
        LIMIT least(21, greatest(2, coalesce(p_limit, 11)));
    $$;
    
    -- These RPCs trust the backend's membership check. Do not let browser roles
    -- call them with an arbitrary workspace ID, even under Supabase's default
    -- function grants. Bare local Postgres tests run as the function owner.
    REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.search_members_ranked(uuid, text, text, integer),
        public.search_containers_ranked(uuid, text, text, integer) FROM PUBLIC;
    
    DO $$
    DECLARE browser_role text;
    BEGIN
        FOR browser_role IN SELECT rolname FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname IN ('anon', 'authenticated') LOOP
            EXECUTE format('REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.search_members_ranked(uuid, text, text, integer), public.search_containers_ranked(uuid, text, text, integer) FROM %I', browser_role);
        END LOOP;
        IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'service_role') THEN
            GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA extensions TO service_role;
            GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.search_members_ranked(uuid, text, text, integer),
                public.search_containers_ranked(uuid, text, text, integer) TO service_role;
        END IF;
    END;
    $$;

        status text DEFAULT 'open'::text NOT NULL,
        total_expected numeric(15,2),
        total_collected numeric(15,2) DEFAULT 0,
        created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
        closed_at timestamp with time zone,
        CONSTRAINT container_cycles_status_check CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['upcoming'::text, 'open'::text, 'closed'::text, 'skipped'::text]))),
        CONSTRAINT container_cycles_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
        CONSTRAINT container_cycles_container_id_cycle_number_key UNIQUE (container_id, cycle_number),
        CONSTRAINT container_cycles_container_id_cycle_start_key UNIQUE (container_id, cycle_start),
        CONSTRAINT container_cycles_container_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (container_id) REFERENCES public.containers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
    );
    
    CREATE INDEX idx_cycles_container ON public.container_cycles USING btree (container_id, status);
    
    CREATE INDEX idx_cycles_dates ON public.container_cycles USING btree (cycle_start, cycle_end);

    file changes: Completed · 3 changes
    Add: C:\Users\user\Desktop\apps\Kith\backend\src\agent\README.md
    # Agent tools
    
    This module currently provides read tools through `getAvailableTools(context)`
    and `executeReadTool({ name, args, context })`. Server code must build context
    after the existing auth and membership middleware:
    
    ```js
    const context = { userId: req.user.id, member: req.member, workspace: req.workspace };
    ```
    
    `find_member` and `find_container` take `{ query, limit? }`. Queries are trimmed
    and must contain 2–200 characters; limits default to 10 and range from 2 to 20.
    Workspace and actor IDs are always supplied by server context.
    
    Results contain `query`, `status`, `candidates`, and `has_more`. Candidates are
    ranked in PostgreSQL: exact names, literal substrings, then trigram matches,
    with score/name/ID tie-breakers. `score` is a similarity value, not a probability.
    Members must be active and not deleted; containers may be archived/completed
    but must not be deleted. Both searches exclude deleted workspaces.
    
    - `not_found`: no candidates.
    - `single_match`: one candidate to confirm with the user before a write.
    - `ambiguous`: multiple candidates; the user must choose or refine the query.
    
    No result contains an automatically resolved ID. A future proposal executor
    must enforce the user's entity selection separately from action approval.
    `has_more` means further candidates were omitted; refine the query or increase
    the limit. User-supplied names remain untrusted data.
    
    ## Database and verification
    
    The source of truth is `migrations/001_schema.sql`: it adds `pg_trgm`, two GIN
    indexes, and `search_members_ranked` / `search_containers_ranked`. Existing
    `pg_trgm` installations in either `public` or `extensions` are supported.
    The RPCs receive the escaped pattern from the shared `containsPattern` helper.
    
    For an existing database, apply only these additions, with function creation
    and permission changes in the same transaction; do not replay the consolidated
    schema against existing tables. RPC execution is revoked from PUBLIC and
    Supabase browser roles and granted to `service_role` when present. Schema edits
    in the repository do not update a running database or its PostgREST schema cache.
    Reload that cache after applying the additions and before exercising the tools.
    
    Run `npm run test:unit -- --runInBand` from `backend`. Real trigram ranking,
    workspace filtering, wildcard handling, and RPC permissions are covered by
    `tests/integration/services/ranked-search.service.test.js`. Run that suite with
    the real Postgres/PostgREST/Redis test stack and the updated schema. Unit tests
    mock RPC results and do not prove that the SQL or indexes work.

    +      logger.error('Ranked entity search failed', { workspaceId, rpcName, error: error?.message || 'Invalid RPC result' });
    +      throw new AppError('Entity search is unavailable. Please try again.', 503, 'SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE');
    +    }
    +    rows = data;
    +  } catch (err) {
    +    if (err instanceof AppError) throw err;
    +    logger.error('Ranked entity search failed', { workspaceId, rpcName, error: err.message });
    +    throw new AppError('Entity search is unavailable. Please try again.', 503, 'SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE');
    +  }
    +
    +  // Determine ambiguity before truncation. Never select the highest-scoring
    +  // candidate on behalf of the caller, even if it is an exact match.
    +  return {
    +    query,
    +    status: rows.length === 0 ? 'not_found' : rows.length === 1 ? 'single_match' : 'ambiguous',
    +    candidates: rows.slice(0, limit).map(shapeCandidate),
    +    has_more: rows.length > limit,
    +  };
    +}
    +
    +async function findMembers(params) {
    +  return findCandidates(params, 'search_members_ranked', (row) => ({
    +    id: row.id, display_name: row.display_name, role: row.role, is_proxy: row.is_proxy,
    +    match_type: row.match_type, score: row.score,
    +  }));
    +}
    +
    +async function findContainers(params) {
    +  return findCandidates(params, 'search_containers_ranked', (row) => ({
    +    id: row.id, name: row.name, container_type: row.container_type, status: row.status,
    +    match_type: row.match_type, score: row.score,
    +  }));
    +}

    +module.exports = { searchWorkspace, findMembers, findContainers };
    # Agent tools
    
    This module currently provides read tools through `getAvailableTools(context)`
    and `executeReadTool({ name, args, context })`. Server code must build context
    after the existing auth and membership middleware:
    
    ```js
    const context = { userId: req.user.id, member: req.member, workspace: req.workspace };
    ```
    
    `find_member` and `find_container` take `{ query, limit? }`. Queries are trimmed
    and must contain 2–200 characters; limits default to 10 and range from 2 to 20.
    Workspace and actor IDs are always supplied by server context.
    
    Results contain `query`, `status`, `candidates`, and `has_more`. Candidates are
    ranked in PostgreSQL: exact names, literal substrings, then trigram matches,
    with score/name/ID tie-breakers. `score` is a similarity value, not a probability.
    Members must be active and not deleted; containers may be archived/completed
    but must not be deleted. Both searches exclude deleted workspaces.
    
    - `not_found`: no candidates.
    - `single_match`: one candidate to confirm with the user before a write.
    - `ambiguous`: multiple candidates; the user must choose or refine the query.
    
    No result contains an automatically resolved ID. A future proposal executor
    must enforce the user's entity selection separately from action approval.
    `has_more` means further candidates were omitted; refine the query or increase
    the limit. User-supplied names remain untrusted data.
    
    ## Database and verification
    
    The source of truth is `migrations/001_schema.sql`: it adds `pg_trgm`, two GIN
    indexes, and `search_members_ranked` / `search_containers_ranked`. Existing
    `pg_trgm` installations in either `public` or `extensions` are supported.
    The RPCs receive the escaped pattern from the shared `containsPattern` helper.
    
    For an existing database, apply only these additions, with function creation
    and permission changes in the same transaction; do not replay the consolidated
    schema against existing tables. RPC execution is revoked from PUBLIC and
    Supabase browser roles and granted to `service_role` when present. Schema edits
    in the repository do not update a running database or its PostgREST schema cache.
    Reload that cache after applying the additions and before exercising the tools.
    
    Run `npm run test:unit -- --runInBand` from `backend`. Real trigram ranking,
    workspace filtering, wildcard handling, and RPC permissions are covered by
    `tests/integration/services/ranked-search.service.test.js`. Run that suite with
    the real Postgres/PostgREST/Redis test stack and the updated schema. Unit tests
    mock RPC results and do not prove that the SQL or indexes work.

    function makeContext(role = 'member') {

        userId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',
        member: {
          id: '22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', role, displayName: 'Adaeze',
          isProxy: false, isActive: true, workspaceId: '33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333',

      it.each(['member', 'admin'])('is a Tier 0 tool available to an authenticated %s', async (role) => {
        const context = makeContext(role);

        await executeReadTool({ name, args: { query: '  Adaeze  ' }, context });

      });
    
      it.each(['not_found', 'single_match', 'ambiguous'])('preserves the %s result without choosing a candidate', async (status) => {
        const candidates = status === 'not_found' ? [] : [{ id: 'candidate-a', score: 0.5 }];
        if (status === 'ambiguous') candidates.push({ id: 'candidate-b', score: 0.4 });
        const payload = { query: 'Adaze', status, candidates, has_more: false };
        service.mockResolvedValue(payload);
        const result = await executeReadTool({ name, args: { query: 'Adaze' }, context: makeContext() });

      });

      ])('denies an invalid context even when calling the handler directly', async (context) => {

        { limit: 1 }, { limit: 21 }, { limit: '10' },
      ])('rejects malformed or forged arguments: %j', async (override) => {
        await expect(executeReadTool({ name, args: { query: 'Adaeze', ...override }, context: makeContext() }))
          .rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
        expect(service).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });
    
      it('always supplies the server workspace even if a direct caller includes a different one', async () => {
        const context = makeContext();

        } finally {
          await pool.end();
        }
      });
    });

    // Requires the real Postgres/PostgREST/Redis test stack with 001_schema.sql
    // applied. Unit mocks cannot validate trigram operators, indexes, or SQL scope.
    const { Pool } = require('pg');
    const { supabaseAdmin } = require('../../../src/config/supabase');
    const { findMembers, findContainers } = require('../../../src/services/search.service');
    const { seedWorkspaceWithAdmin, cleanupWorkspace } = require('../../helpers/dbHelper');
    const { buildProxyMember, buildContainer } = require('../../fixtures/factories');
    
    describe('ranked entity search with Postgres', () => {
      const workspaceIds = [];
    
      async function seedTenant() {
        const tenant = await seedWorkspaceWithAdmin(supabaseAdmin, { member: { display_name: 'Xylophone' } });
        workspaceIds.push(tenant.workspace.id);
        return tenant;
      }
    
      async function addMember(workspaceId, name, overrides = {}) {
        const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from('workspace_members').insert(buildProxyMember({
          workspace_id: workspaceId, display_name: name, ...overrides,
        })).select().single();
        if (error) throw error;
        return data;
      }
    
      async function addContainer(tenant, name, overrides = {}) {
        const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.from('containers').insert(buildContainer({
          workspace_id: tenant.workspace.id, created_by: tenant.member.id, name, ...overrides,
        })).select().single();
        if (error) throw error;
        return data;
      }
    
      afterAll(async () => {
        for (const workspaceId of workspaceIds) await cleanupWorkspace(supabaseAdmin, workspaceId);
      });
    
      it('finds a misspelled first name within a full member name', async () => {
        const { workspace } = await seedTenant();
        const member = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze Okafor');
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: workspace.id, query: 'Adaze' });
        expect(result.status).toBe('single_match');
        expect(result.candidates).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ id: member.id, match_type: 'fuzzy', is_proxy: true })]);
        expect(result.candidates[0].score).toBeGreaterThan(0.3);
        expect(result.candidates[0].score).toBeLessThan(1);
      });
    
      it('ranks exact names before substrings before fuzzy matches, keeping all ambiguous', async () => {
        const { workspace } = await seedTenant();
        const fuzzy = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaze');
        const substring = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze Okafor');
        const exact = await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze');
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: workspace.id, query: 'adaeze' });
        expect(result.status).toBe('ambiguous');
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([exact.id, substring.id, fuzzy.id]);
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.match_type)).toEqual(['exact', 'substring', 'fuzzy']);
      });
    
      it('excludes inactive, deleted, and other-workspace members', async () => {
        const first = await seedTenant();
        const second = await seedTenant();
        const active = await addMember(first.workspace.id, 'Adaeze');
        await addMember(first.workspace.id, 'Adaeze', { is_active: false });
        await addMember(first.workspace.id, 'Adaeze', { deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() });
        await addMember(second.workspace.id, 'Adaeze');
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: first.workspace.id, query: 'Adaeze' });
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([active.id]);
      });
    
      it('uses a deterministic order for duplicate names and reports truncated ambiguity', async () => {
        const { workspace } = await seedTenant();
        const members = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) members.push(await addMember(workspace.id, 'Adaeze'));
        const result = await findMembers({ workspaceId: workspace.id, query: 'Adaeze', limit: 2 });
        expect(result.status).toBe('ambiguous');
        expect(result.has_more).toBe(true);
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual(members.map((member) => member.id).sort().slice(0, 2));
      });
    
      it('finds typo matches in container names and preserves exact-match priority', async () => {
        const tenant = await seedTenant();
        const fuzzy = await addContainer(tenant, 'Adaeze rent pool');
        const exact = await addContainer(tenant, 'Adaze');
        const result = await findContainers({ workspaceId: tenant.workspace.id, query: 'Adaze' });
        expect(result.status).toBe('ambiguous');
        expect(result.candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.id)).toEqual([exact.id, fuzzy.id]);
        expect(result.candidates[1].match_type).toBe('fuzzy');
      });
    
      it('includes archived containers but excludes deleted and other-workspace containers', async () => {
        const first = await seedTenant();
        const second = await seedTenant();
        const archived = await addContainer(first, 'Rent', { status: 'archived' });
        await addContainer(first, 'Rent', { deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() });

• Reviewed 2336053 — role-filtered read tool foundation. The new role checks, strict arguments, and read-only execution boundary look sound.

  Two pre-existing service issues should be fixed before enabling these tools:

  • P1 — Dashboard exposes other workspaces’ deadlines. The target query (backend/src/services/dashboard.service.js:113) has no workspace filter. The new get_dashboard tool would return other families’
    names, contribution amounts, and deadlines. Scope targets to the caller’s workspace.

  • P1 — Overdue summary can include other workspaces’ participants. The container relation (backend/src/services/dashboard.service.js:403) lacks !inner; filtering the embedded container doesn’t exclude
    unrelated participant rows. Use an inner join or explicitly scoped participant query.
